- **maintainability-auditor**: Use this agent for an ambitious, structural code-quality audit that looks beyond the diff for ways to make the implementation dramatically simpler. It hunts for "code judo" moves (restructurings that preserve behavior while deleting whole categories of complexity), file-size sprawl, spaghetti-branch growth, and weak seams that make the code hard to test or change. Triggers on: "execute the plan", "run the tasks", "orchestrate", or automatically after decomposition completes. &lt;example&gt; user: "The architect created the task graph. Execute it." assistant: "Invoking the orchestrator to validate, schedule, and execute the task DAG." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/mao-marketplace](https://github.com/aiconnai/mao-marketplace/blob/HEAD/npm/plugin/agents/mao-orchestrator.md) · 2 copies)
- **mao-reflector**: Performs meta-analysis after complex multi-agent runs. Evaluates whether the combined result solves the original problem. Identifies patterns for future model routing optimization. Only invoke for runs with 8+ tasks or when explicitly requested. &lt;example&gt; user: "Reflect on how the implementation went" assistant: "Using the reflector for meta-analysis of this run." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/mao-marketplace](https://github.com/aiconnai/mao-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-agent-orchestrator/agents/mao-reflector.md))
- **mao-reviewer**: Performs cross-agent code review on completed tasks. Analyzes code for security, performance, design quality, and completeness. Creates correction tasks when issues are found. Used automatically by the orchestrator after verification passes. &lt;example&gt; user: "Review the fraud detection implementation" assistant: "Using the reviewer agent for a thorough code review." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/mao-marketplace](https://github.com/aiconnai/mao-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-agent-orchestrator/agents/mao-reviewer.md))
- **mao-verifier**: Runs deterministic verification pipelines on completed tasks: type checking, unit tests, linting, and formatting. Reports pass/fail with exact error context. Used automatically by the orchestrator after each task completes. &lt;example&gt; user: "Verify the auth middleware implementation" assistant: "Running verification pipeline via the verifier agent." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/mao-marketplace](https://github.com/aiconnai/mao-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-agent-orchestrator/agents/mao-verifier.md))
- **mao-worker**: Executes mechanical and low-complexity tasks efficiently: CRUD endpoints, database migrations, boilerplate code, file moves/renames, import updates, simple documentation, type definitions, configuration files, and test scaffolding. Tasks scoring 0-3 on complexity. Used automatically by the orchestrator for haiku-tier tasks. &lt;example&gt; user: "Create the database migration for the users table" assistant: "Simple migration task. Using the worker agent." &lt;/example&gt; ([aiconnai/mao-marketplace](https://github.com/aiconnai/mao-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-agent-orchestrator/agents/mao-worker.md))
